To this end, we introduce HiMed, a Hindi reasoning medical corpus and benchmark suite covering both Western and Indian medicine. We further propose HiMed-8B, a Hindi-form medical reasoning LLM, through the design of decaying scaffolding reward. Extensive experiments demonstrate improvement in Hindi medical reasoning performance and reduction in the English--Hindi accuracy gap. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each training stage and reward component. All data and code are available on GitHub: https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/HiMed.

</details>


### [37] [Know You Before You Speak: User-State Modeling for LLM Personalization in Multi-Turn Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24647)
*Jiani Luo,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Shuyi Miao,Bingbing Xu,Stefan Konigorski,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUMA框架基于自由能原理，将个性化对话建模为部分可观测条件下的决策过程，通过显式用户状态模型捕捉潜在用户状态及其动作条件动态，并通过最小化期望自由能来平衡认知和实用目标，从被动记忆检索转向基于模型的用户演化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆和档案的个性化对话方法主要重用可观测的用户信息，难以建模用户状态的动态变化，也无法根据对话动作对未来用户状态的影响来选择动作。

Method: 提出PUMA框架，基于自由能原理(FEP)，维护用户隐藏状态的后验信念，细化用户状态模型以生成观测和预测动作条件状态转移，并通过最小化期望自由能选择对话动作，统一平衡认知目标和实用目标。

Result: 在心理健康领域的咨询和动机性访谈基准上，PUMA改善了长程对话效果，保持了强响应质量；跨数据集实验表明其具有更可靠的用户状态估计和下一状态预测能力。

Conclusion: 个性化对话应从被动记忆检索转向基于模型的用户演化决策，PUMA通过显式建模用户状态动态和前瞻性动作选择实现了这一转变。

Abstract: Personalized dialogue requires more than recalling explicit user histories: systems also need to infer hidden user states that evolve through interaction and shape appropriate response strategies. Existing memory- and profile-based methods primarily reuse observable user information, offering limited support for modeling user-state dynamics or selecting actions based on how they shape future user states. We propose PUMA (Prospective User-state Modeling for Action selection), a framework grounded in the Free Energy Principle (FEP) that formulates personalization as decision-making under partial observability, centered on an explicit user state model that captures latent user states and their action-conditioned dynamics. At each turn, PUMA maintains a belief over the user's hidden state, refines the user state model for observation generation and action-conditioned state transition, and selects dialogue actions by minimizing expected free energy, balancing epistemic and pragmatic objectives under a unified criterion. This formulation shifts personalization from passive memory retrieval to model-based decision-making over user evolution. We instantiate PUMA on healthcare-oriented counseling and motivational interviewing benchmarks with latent state annotations for rigorous evaluation. Experiments show that PUMA improves long-horizon dialogue outcomes while maintaining strong response quality, and a cross-dataset study demonstrates more reliable user-state estimation and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [38] [Mix-MoE: Improving Multilingual Machine Translation of Large Language Models through Mixed MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24681)
*Bo Li,Tianyu Dong,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Mix-MoE框架，通过混合专家模型将语言模型专家和机器翻译专家分离，结合傅里叶变换增强路由机制，解决多语言机器翻译中微调大语言模型的参数干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多语言机器翻译中表现优异，但使用平行语料微调会引发严重的参数干扰问题，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出Mix-MoE框架，包含两个阶段：单语语料上的MoE后预训练和平行语料上的MoE后预训练。将MoE层分为语言模型专家（LM Experts）和机器翻译专家（MT Experts），分别保留单语知识和学习双语翻译知识。引入基于傅里叶变换特征增强的路由机制促进专家间交互。

Result: Mix-MoE在多语言机器翻译上显著优于现有基线方法，有效缓解了参数干扰问题。

Conclusion: 通过专业化的专家分离设计和傅里叶增强路由机制，Mix-MoE成功克服了多语言翻译中的参数干扰挑战，为利用LLM进行多语言机器翻译提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great promise in multilingual machine translation (MT), even with limited bilingual supervision. However, fine-tuning LLMs with parallel corpora presents major challenges, namely parameter interference. To address these issues, we propose Mix-MoE, a mixed Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to train LLMs for multilingual MT. Our framework operates in two distinct stages: (1) post-pretraining with MoE on monolingual corpora, and (2) post-pretraining with MoE on parallel corpora. Crucially, we divide the MoE layers into two specialized groups: Language Model Experts (LM Experts) and Machine Translation Experts (MT Experts). LM Experts are designed to capture and retain the monolingual knowledge learned by the pre-trained LLM. MT Experts, on the other hand, are specifically trained to acquire and store bilingual translation knowledge. Furthermore, to facilitate effective interaction between these specialized experts and leverage potential underlying structural patterns in text, we introduce a routing mechanism enhanced by Fourier Transform features derived from model representations. The experimental results demonstrate that Mix-MoE excels in multilingual MT, significantly outperforming existing baselines and showing notable progress in mitigating parameter interference.

</details>


### [39] [CP-Agent: A Calibrated Risk-Controlled Agent for Feedback-Driven Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24693)
*Peisong Wang,Bowen Liu,Zehua Li,Yuyao Wang,Zhiwei Ma,Yuhan Li,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-Agent，通过执行反馈驱动的校准停止过程，在不更新参数的情况下将LLM在竞赛级编程任务上的Pass@1从25.8%提升至48.5%，并处于成本-准确率效率前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在竞赛级编程中表现不足，而现有智能体解决方案过度依赖大规模推理采样或昂贵的多阶段后训练，缺乏对执行反馈何时有效及其作用机制的理论理解。

Method: 将反馈驱动求解建模为校准停止过程，识别三个关键量：错误接受风险、程序级证据和活跃状态成功风险；并实例化三种机制：双粒度验证、测试增强和经验驱动自我进化，组合为CP-Agent。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench Pro上Pass@1从25.8%提升至48.5%，在ICPC-Eval上Refine@5提升11.0%，在三个LLM骨干上均处于成本-准确率效率前沿。

Conclusion: 通过理论建模指导的轻量级机制设计，无需参数更新即可显著提升竞赛级编程性能，且各组件分别对应影响所识别的理论量。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with contest-level programming, while many agentic remedies rely on massive inference-time sampling or expensive multi-stage post-training. We study when execution feedback reliably helps an LLM CP solver and which mechanisms govern the gains. We model feedback-driven solving as a calibrated stopped process and identify three quantities: false-admission risk, program-level evidence against bad programs, and the active-state success hazard. Under held-out trace calibration and selection from a pre-declared finite controller manifest, the resulting structural certificate lower-bounds the clean success probability before false admission. We instantiate mechanisms targeting these quantities as Dual-Granularity Verification, Test Augmentation, and Experience-Driven Self-Evolving, yielding CP-Agent. Without updating any parameters, CP-Agent raises Pass@1 from 25.8\% to 48.5\% on LiveCodeBench Pro and improves Refine@5 by 11.0\% on ICPC-Eval. Across three LLM backbones, CP-Agent lies on the cost--accuracy efficiency frontier, and ablations show that each component primarily affects its corresponding certificate quantity.

</details>


### [40] [The Path Matters: Learning a Token-Commitment Policy for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24697)
*Bohang Sun,Max Zhu,Francesco Caso,Jindong Gu,Junchi Yu,Philip Torr,Pietro Liò,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceLock 是一个轻量级可插拔控制器，通过学习可复用的令牌提交策略（token commitment policy），为冻结的扩散语言模型在并行解码过程中决定哪些提议令牌应被提交到部分解码序列中，从而改善质量-步数权衡，并在跨设置部署下保持稳定表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型通过并行细化多个令牌位置来加速生成，但并行性引入了一个隐藏的控制问题：每一步该将哪些提议令牌转移到部分解码序列中（即令牌提交决策）。现有方法依赖手工设计的置信度规则或模块特定的接受过滤器，缺乏可学习的通用策略。

Method: TraceLock 将令牌提交建模为可复用的轨迹状态策略（trace-state policy）。它从未来稳定性中获取自监督信号：在解码步 t，若提议令牌与完整解码轨迹结束后该位置的最终令牌一致，则标记为稳定。控制器对变长轨迹状态进行评分，决定哪些活跃令牌提议应被提交。训练完成后，该控制器可直接跨局部窗口宽度、生成长度和步数预算部署，无需重新训练或逐设置校准。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和代码生成任务上，TraceLock 在质量-步数权衡上优于启发式方法和学习基线，在跨设置部署下表现出特别稳定的行为。诊断分析表明，其决策不能简化为标量置信度，说明冻结扩散语言模型暴露了一个超越置信度解码的可学习提交轨迹空间。

Conclusion: 令牌提交是可学习的策略问题，TraceLock 提供了一种有效的解决方案，证明了冻结扩散语言模型存在超越简单置信度阈值的可学习提交轨迹空间。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models promise faster generation by refining many token positions in parallel, but this parallelism introduces a hidden control problem: which proposed tokens should be transferred into the partially decoded sequence at each step? We refer to this decision as token commitment. Existing frozen-generator decoders largely rely on hand-designed confidence rules or block-specific acceptance filters. We argue that token commitment can instead be learned as a reusable trace-state policy. We introduce TraceLock, a lightweight plug-in controller that instantiates this policy for a frozen diffusion language model. Since oracle commitment times are unavailable, TraceLock derives self-supervision from future stability: at decoding step t, a proposed token for position i is labeled stable if it matches the final token at position i after the full decoding trace completes. The controller scores variable-length trace states and decides which active token proposals should be committed to the partially decoded sequence. Once trained for a given frozen backbone, the controller can be deployed across local-window widths, generation lengths, and step budgets without retraining or per-setting calibration. Experiments on question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation show that TraceLock improves the quality-step tradeoff over heuristic and learned baselines, with particularly stable behavior under cross-setting deployment. Diagnostic analyses show that its decisions are not reducible to scalar confidence, suggesting that frozen diffusion language models expose a learnable space of commitment trajectories beyond confidence-based decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/BobSun98/TraceLock.

</details>


### [41] [TS-Skill: A Benchmark for Evaluating Analytical Skills in Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24703)
*Liying Han,Kang Yang,Oliver Wang,Jason Wu,Pengrui Quan,Gaofeng Dong,Ozan Baris Mulayim,Sizhe Ma,Yuyang Yuan,Dezhi Hong,Mario Berges,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-Skill基准，用于评估时间序列问答（TSQA）中三种可组合的分析技能：时间尺度选择（SK1）、时间定位（SK2）和跨区间整合（SK3）。通过SKEvol框架自动构建基准，实验发现现有LLM和TSLM在SK1-SK3上存在显著且不均衡的能力差距，其中SK3对非智能体模型最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSQA基准通常按任务类型或高层推理类别组织，难以诊断模型在信号层面（如不同尺度的模式、特定时间位置、跨区间整合）的底层能力缺陷。

Method: 提出TS-Skill基准，包含三种技能（SK1/SK2/SK3）；开发SKEvol框架实现自动化构建，包括领域感知时间序列种子生成、技能控制的问题生成、元数据和代码辅助的答案构建、多阶段信号验证及人工循环审核。

Result: 在10个先进LLM和TSLM上的实验显示，模型在SK1-SK3上存在显著且不均衡的能力差距；SK3对非智能体模型始终具有挑战性，而工具增强的智能体在独立SK3上表现出选择性优势。

Conclusion: 技能级评估能够揭示被聚合TSQA分数掩盖的时间推理失败模式，支持更精细的诊断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and time-series language models (TSLMs) are increasingly applied to time-series question answering (TSQA). Unlike text-only QA, TSQA requires models to ground answers in temporal signals whose patterns may occur at different scales, specific time locations, or across separated intervals. However, existing benchmarks are typically organized by task types or high-level reasoning categories, making it difficult to diagnose the underlying signal-level capabilities driving model performance. We introduce TS-Skill, a controlled benchmark for evaluating three composable analytical skills in TSQA: temporal scale selection (SK1), temporal localization (SK2), and cross-interval integration (SK3). TS-Skill provides timestamp-aware questions, broad domain coverage, and human-validated QA quality. To construct the benchmark at scale, we develop SKEvol, a skill-guided agentic framework that combines domain-aware time-series seed generation, skill-controlled question generation, metadata- and code-assisted answer construction, multi-phase signal-grounded verification, and human-in-the-loop curation. Experiments on ten state-of-the-art LLMs and TSLMs reveal substantial and uneven capability gaps across SK1-SK3. In particular, SK3 remains consistently challenging for non-agent models, whereas tool-augmented agents show a selective advantage on standalone SK3. These findings demonstrate that skill-level evaluation can uncover temporal reasoning failures that are obscured by aggregate TSQA scores.

</details>

*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统测量了10个基础模型在25种欧洲语言上的分词器繁殖率（tokens/word），发现从英语（1.2）到希腊语/马耳他语（~3.1）存在2.5倍的"分词器税"差异，遵循罗曼语族→日耳曼语族→斯拉夫语族→乌拉尔语系/波罗的语族的递增层级，且该排名跨领域高度稳定（rho>0.97）。乌克兰语因预训练数据代表性不足，比同源斯拉夫语言多支付15-18%的代价。高繁殖率分词器会破坏形态学边界，而跨语言少样本学习的表现差异源于模型本身而非语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示非英语语言在分词过程中隐含的"分词器税"（即更高的token消耗），量化不同语言在基础模型中的处理效率差异，特别是乌克兰语等代表性不足语言的额外代价。

Method: 在平行文本上测量10个基础模型对25种欧洲语言的分词器繁殖率（tokens/word），进行跨领域（三种文本类型）稳定性分析，并通过子词分析和跨语言少样本评估探究其成因与影响。

Result: 繁殖率从英语1.2到希腊语/马耳他语约3.1，跨语言层级稳定；乌克兰语比同源语言高15-18%；繁殖率排名跨领域高度相关（rho>0.97）；高繁殖率分词器破坏形态边界；少样本效果由模型本身决定而非语言。

Conclusion: 分词器繁殖率是非英语NLP的隐藏成本，受预训练数据代表性影响显著，且该成本在各文本领域一致；模型在处理不同语言时的性能差异主要源于模型内部机制，而非目标语言特性。

Abstract: Tokenizer fertility the number of tokens per word imposes a hidden cost on non-English NLP. We measure fertility for ten foundation models across 25 European languages on parallel text, producing the first controlled tokenizer tax map for the continent. The tax spans 2.5x from English (1.2 tokens/word) to Greek/Maltese (~3.1), following a clear hierarchy: Romance (1.5-1.7), Germanic (1.7-1.9), Slavic (2.2-2.5), Uralic/Baltic (2.7-3.0). Ukrainian (2.7) pays 15-18% more than cognate Slavic languages, reflecting underrepresentation in pre-training data. Fertility rankings are domain-invariant across three text registers (rho > 0.97). A subword analysis reveals that high-fertility tokenizers fragment morphological boundaries rather than preserving them. Cross-lingual few-shot evaluation on four Slavic languages shows that few-shot effects are model-intrinsic, not language-dependent. We release all measurements as a public dataset.

</details>

Method: 基于神经符号架构，使用开源模型Llama 3 70B和闭源模型Gemini 1.5 Flash，在英语和西班牙语两种语言下进行实验。设计两个精心评估的场景，邀请8名参与者进行测试，评估状态变换作为玩家表达催化剂的效果。

Conclusion: 神经符号结合的转换方法能有效缓解纯LLM叙事的不连贯问题，同时不牺牲玩家表达的自由度，为交互式叙事系统提供了一种可行的混合设计范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have changed the possibilities of Interactive Storytelling systems that process free-text user input. However, as more of these systems are built, evidence continues to mount regarding the story coherence problems that arise when relying solely on them. Recent research suggests that LLMs can effectively predict state changes within rule-based Interactive Storytelling systems, triggering pre-programmed world-state transformations.
  In this paper, we conduct an exploratory evaluation of whether such transformations can serve as a catalyst for player expression while aiming to address the incoherence issues typical of purely LLM-based approaches. Building upon a neuro-symbolic architecture, we conducted experiments using an open-source model (Llama 3 70B) and a closed-source model (Gemini 1.5 Flash), with testing conducted in both English and Spanish. Eight participants played two scenarios, carefully designed to assess different evaluation objectives. Our observations suggest that transformations offer a way to maintain world-state consistency while encouraging players to interact creatively through their written inputs.

</details>

Conclusion: ROC分析是翻译质量评估系统评价的有效工具，在保持结果一致性的同时，能够提供可操作的性能洞察，支持商业决策。

Abstract: The increasing use of automated translation quality estimation (QE) systems calls for practical, decision-oriented methods for evaluating their performance. We propose that Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) analysis is a useful approach for this purpose. Our study shows that ROC analysis not only produces results consistent with currently prevalent methods, but also offers several important advantages, including actionable performance insights that support business decision-making.

</details>


### [45] [StepGap: A Hybrid NLI-LLM Checker for Step-Level Evidence-Gap Detectionin Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24733)
*Yuelyu Ji,Zhuochun Li,Hui Ji,Daqing He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StepGap是一个混合NLI-LLM决策树，用于检测多跳问答中的步骤级证据缺口，并输出三类标签（矛盾主张、无关证据、缺失桥梁），在步骤级F1上达到72.0，并通过消融实验揭示LLM基线存在“竞争性错误抵消”现象。作为GRPO过程奖励，StepGap将Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的精确匹配率从32.1提升至35.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中现有的证据缺口检测方法缺乏细粒度的步骤级诊断能力，且LLM基线存在不可分解的“竞争性错误抵消”问题，即内部阶段相互掩盖错误，导致性能评估失真。同时，问题级F1指标存在机械性膨胀的“Q-F1陷阱”，需要更可靠的步骤级诊断。

Method: 提出StepGap混合决策树，结合NLI与LLM进行多阶段推理，输出三类步骤级标签：矛盾主张（CC）、无关证据（IE）、缺失桥梁（MB），每类标签对应具体的修复操作。通过消融实验验证各阶段贡献，并暴露Q-F1陷阱。将StepGap作为类型化的GRPO过程奖励信号，用于优化Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型。

Result: 在82个多跳问题（181个标注步骤，κ=0.704）上，StepGap达到sF1=72.0，处于LLM基线（70.1）的bootstrap置信区间内，但结构更可分解：每个阶段移除均降低F1，而LLM基线四个阶段中有三个移除后F1反而提升，揭示了竞争性错误抵消。作为GRPO过程奖励，StepGap将Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的EM从32.1±0.3提升至35.4±0.9，单次运行相比Search-R1 GRPO复现获得+5.6 Avg EM增益。

Conclusion: StepGap提供了可分解、可解释的步骤级证据缺口检测方案，有效避免了LLM基线的竞争性错误抵消问题，并作为过程奖励信号显著提升了下游模型的推理能力。步骤级F1应成为多跳QA诊断的标准指标。

Abstract: We present \textbf{StepGap}, a hybrid NLI-LLM decision tree that detects step-level evidence gaps in multi-hop QA and emits one of three typed labels: \textsc{Contradicted Claim} (CC), \textsc{Irrelevant Evidence} (IE), or \textsc{Missing Bridge} (MB), each tied to a concrete repair action. On 82 multi-hop questions (181 annotated steps, $κ{=}0.704$), StepGap reaches sF1$=$72.0, within the bootstrap confidence interval of an LLM-only baseline (70.1) but with a more decomposable structure: every StepGap stage \emph{hurts} F1 when removed, while three of four LLM-only removals \emph{improve} F1 -- a sign of \emph{competing-error cancellation}, where internal stages mask each other's errors. We further expose a \emph{Q-F1 trap}: question-level F1 is mechanically inflated by checkers that flag every step, making step-level F1 the necessary diagnostic. Used as a typed GRPO process reward, StepGap improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct Exact Match from $32.1{\pm}0.3$ to $35.4{\pm}0.9$ across three seeds, with the single-run comparison showing a $+5.6$ Avg EM gain over the matched Search-R1 GRPO reproduction.

</details>


### [46] [Who judges the judges? Governance from metrics: a runtime framework for continuous LLM compliance monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24737)
*Jehanne Dussert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于指标的治理"原则，通过运行时可观测性将合规转化为连续信号，并实现govllm开源框架——采用监管法官面板（SLM评估器）进行治理驱动的路由选择，在49对标注数据上验证了多模型组合优于单一模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI合规方法将合规视为二元的、审计时刻的静态判断，这种"合规虚构"在结构上无法满足EU AI Act对持续人工监督和运行时行为漂移检测的要求。

Method: 提出governance from metrics原则，构建govllm框架：采用治理驱动的路由架构，基于累积合规分数而非延迟或成本选择模型；设计由专用LLM评估器组成的监管法官面板，将法官间分歧重新定义为需要人工仲裁的监管不确定性信号；使用4个小型本地SLM（1.7B-7B参数）在49对标注数据上评估5项监管标准。

Result: 模型间一致率从51.5%（mistral:7b）到69.1%（phi4-mini）不等，无单一模型在所有标准上占优；发现小型监管法官的三种结构性失效模式，以及特定模型的位置偏差（在三种问题顺序条件下使一致率下降高达25个百分点）。

Conclusion: 多模型组合（Profile-as-jury）设计优于单一法官，govllm作为开源框架支持可复现的AI治理研究，但小型模型在监管评估中存在显著的结构性局限。

Abstract: Current approaches to AI compliance treat conformity as a binary, audit-time verdict rather than a continuous, measurable property of production systems. We argue that this compliance fiction is structurally ill-suited to the requirements of the EU AI Act, which demands ongoing human oversight and the detection of emergent behavioural drift in deployed systems. We introduce governance from metrics, a principle whereby regulatory compliance is derived as a continuous signal from runtime observability rather than from static assessments. Building on this principle, we present govllm, an open-source framework implementing a governance-driven routing architecture in which model selection is determined by accumulated compliance scores rather than by latency or cost alone. Central to our approach is a panel of regulatory judges - LLM evaluators specialised per criterion (EU AI Act, GDPR, ANSSI, accessibility) - whose inter-judge disagreement we reframe not as noise but as a regulatory uncertainty signal warranting human arbitration. We validate this approach through a ground truth corpus of 49 annotated prompt/response pairs across five regulatory criteria, evaluated by four small language models (SLMs, 1.7B-7B parameters) running fully on-premise. Agreement rates range from 51.5% (mistral:7b) to 69.1% (phi4-mini), with no single model dominating across all criteria - empirically motivating the Profile-as-jury design. We further document three structural failure modes in small regulatory judges and a judge-specific position bias that degrades agreement by up to 25 percentage points across three question-order conditions (original, reversed, permuted). govllm is released as open-source software to support reproducible AI governance research.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond the Target: From Imitation to Collaboration in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24793)
*Jinze Li,Yixing Xu,Guanchen Li,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Yang Zhang,Xuanwu Yin,Dong Li,Edith C. H. Ngai,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoSpec是一种协作式推测解码方法，通过强化学习训练仲裁策略，在草稿模型与目标模型预测不一致时选择性地接受草稿模型标记，从而在保持加速效果的同时超越纯目标模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码将目标模型视为唯一可靠权威，仅在草稿标记与目标预测完全匹配时才接受。然而实际中草稿模型在部分位置上可能优于目标模型，传统方法忽略了这种协作潜力。

Method: 提出CoSpec框架，使用强化学习训练一个仲裁策略，在草稿与目标模型预测不一致时决定接受哪一方的标记，实现两者在标记层面的协作而非单纯模仿目标模型。

Result: 实验结果表明CoSpec在保持显著加速效果的同时，超越了仅使用目标模型的性能。

Conclusion: CoSpec将推测解码从"模仿"转向"协作"，为推测解码提供了新的视角，证明了在标记级别选择性地采纳草稿模型预测可以同时提升效率和准确性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SPD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by letting a smaller draft model propose multiple future tokens that are verified in parallel by a larger target model. The dominant SPD paradigm treats the target model as the sole reliable teacher, accepting a draft token only when it exactly matches the target prediction. This design implicitly assumes that the target is always the better choice at every position. In practice, this assumption does not hold. Although the draft is the weaker model overall, it is not uniformly inferior at the token level. In a meaningful fraction of cases where draft and target disagree, the draft's choice is the one that leads to the correct final answer. Inspired by this, we introduce \textbf{Collaborative Speculative Decoding (CoSpec)}, a generalization of SPD that no longer treats the target model as the sole token-level authority. CoSpec trains an arbitration policy via reinforcement learning to decide whether to accept tokens from the draft or target model, selectively accepting draft tokens at mismatches when doing so is likely to yield a correct final answer. Experimental results show that CoSpec maintains substantial speedups while surpassing target-only performance. By shifting the emphasis from imitation to collaboration, CoSpec suggests a new perspective on speculative decoding.

</details>


### [48] [Repeated Sequences Reveal Gaps between Large Language Models and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24850)
*Kumiko Tanaka-Ishii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于重复子序列的熵评估框架，通过分析文本中重复子序列的分布及其与Rényi熵的关系，揭示大语言模型生成文本与人类自然语言在长程统计组织上的系统性差异。实验表明，GPT生成文本的熵增长模式随模型规模发生显著偏移，而人类语言则呈现稳定的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法多依赖任务表现或短上下文行为，难以揭示LLM生成文本在长程统计组织层面的结构特征，需一种能探测文本长程重复结构的互补性评估框架。

Method: 基于重复子序列的分布分析，将其与高阶Rényi熵关联，在不同尺度上研究文本对先前建立结构的复用情况。对比人类书面文本与长度匹配的GPT生成文本，采用幂律模型和对数-幂形式对熵增长进行建模。

Result: 人类自然语言在可访问范围内呈现稳定的熵增长模式，个体文本虽有变异但平均行为一致；GPT生成文本的估计指数随模型规模发生系统性且统计显著的偏移。对数-幂形式往往能更好地描述观察到的熵增长。

Conclusion: 重复子序列熵提供了一种定量结构诊断工具，能揭示长程组织上的系统性差异，可在表面流畅性之外区分自然语言与最先进LLM的输出。

Abstract: Evaluating whether large language models (LLMs) capture the structure of natural language beyond local fluency remains an open challenge. Existing evaluation methods, largely based on task performance or short-context behavior, provide limited insight into the long-range statistical organization of generated text.
  We propose a complementary evaluation framework based on repeated subsequences. By analyzing their distribution across scales and relating it to higher-order Rényi entropies, we probe how texts reuse previously established structure under finite-length conditions. Experiments on human-written texts and length-matched GPT-generated texts show that, while power-law models can describe restricted ranges of block length, the observed entropy growth is often equally or better characterized by logarithmic--power forms.
  Across datasets, natural language exhibits stable entropy-growth patterns over accessible ranges, with consistent average behavior despite variability across individual texts. In contrast, GPT-generated texts show systematic and statistically significant shifts in estimated exponents with model size. These results demonstrate that repeated-subsequence entropy provides a quantitative structural diagnostic that reveals systematic differences in long-range organization, distinguishing natural language from state-of-the-art LLM outputs beyond surface-level fluency.

</details>


### [49] [Towards a Universal Causal Reasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24873)
*Qirun Dai,Xiao Liu,Jiawei Zhang,Dylan Zhang,Hao Peng,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UniCo框架，通过覆盖18种因果查询类型并将符号化因果示例转化为代码和自然语言形式，生成高质量因果推理训练数据。在66.6K实例上微调后，Qwen3-4B/8B和Olmo-3-7B-Instruct在分布内任务平均提升22.9%，在7个分布外因果基准上提升8.1%，并在医疗、法律、表格推理等实际任务中展现出更忠实的推理轨迹，忠实度平均提升20.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要聚焦于在特定因果方面对LLM进行基准测试，缺乏能训练通用因果推理器的数据，因果推理训练仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出UniCo数据生成框架，覆盖Pearl因果阶梯上的18种查询类型，将原生符号化示例转化为代码和自然语言形式，利用精确因果推断验证答案并过滤存在推理捷径的案例。

Result: 在66.6K数据上微调后，Qwen3-4B/8B和Olmo-3-7B-Instruct在18种分布内查询类型上平均提升22.9%，在7个分布外基准上超越SOTA方法8.1%；在医疗、法律、表格推理任务中忠实度平均提升20.2%。

Conclusion: 以因果为中心的训练不仅能增强因果推理能力，还能赋予LLM在通用推理任务中的因果思维模式，产生更忠实的推理轨迹。

Abstract: Despite the importance of causal reasoning, training LLMs to reason causally remains underexplored. Existing data efforts mostly focus on benchmarking LLMs on specific aspects of causality, making them less suitable for training generalizable causal reasoners. To address this, we propose UniCo, a data generation framework that both (1) addresses 18 causal query types across Pearl's Causal Ladder and (2) translates natively symbolic examples into code and natural language forms to simulate real-world use cases where causal terms are not explicitly specified. To ensure data quality, UniCo grounds answers with exact causal inference and filters cases with reasoning shortcuts. Upon supervised finetuning with 66.6K UniCo-generated instances, Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Instruct achieve an average of 22.9% improvements across all 18 in-distribution query types, and 8.1% over state-of-the-art causal data generation frameworks on 7 established causal benchmarks outside the training distribution. More importantly, in real-world medical understanding, legal decision, and tabular reasoning, UniCo-trained models consistently display more faithful reasoning traces, outperforming the base models by an average of 20.2% in faithfulness metrics. These suggest that causality-centered training not only strengthens causal reasoning, but also equips LLMs with a causal mindset in general reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [50] [DTO: a Differentiable Training Objective for Effective Counterfactual Story Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24885)
*Amelia Girard,Massimo Piccardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微训练目标（DTO），通过端到端反向传播联合优化重写 fidelity 和语义一致性，在反事实故事改写任务上超越了最大似然基线和偏好学习方法，与当代大语言模型表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实故事改写要求对原文进行极小且高度局部的修改，传统最大似然训练容易忽略这些细微变化，而基于强化学习的训练方法又过于缓慢且难以配置，因此需要一种既高效又能捕捉细粒度改写的训练目标。

Method: 提出可微训练目标（DTO），通过一个完全可微的损失函数对Transformer模型进行端到端微调，该损失函数同时奖励（i）对参考改写的 fidelity 和（ii）与源叙事的语义一致性。

Result: 在TimeTravel和ART数据集上，DTO方法超越了最大似然基线和偏好学习方法，并在所有评估指标上与两个当代大语言模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 任务特定的可微训练目标在细粒度、受控文本生成任务中是有效的，为替代复杂的强化学习方案提供了更简洁高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Counterfactual story rewriting is a natural language processing task that requires updating an existing story to reflect a chosen alternative event, yet preserving all the unaffected storyline elements and overall coherence. While large language models have recently made remarkable progress on this task, it still remains challenging since the required modifications are typically very small in size and highly localized. As a consequence, models trained in a conventional manner with the maximum-likelihood training objective tend to overlook these nuances. At the same time, more sophisticated training approaches based on reinforcement learning are notoriously slow and difficult to set up. For these reasons, our paper proposes a novel, differentiable training objective (DTO) that directly optimizes for the requisite counterfactual improvements. In our approach, a transformer model is fine-tuned via end-to-end backpropagation against a fully differentiable loss function that jointly rewards (i) fidelity to the reference rewrite and (ii) semantic consistency with the source narrative. The empirical evaluation on the TimeTravel and ART datasets shows that the proposed DTO approach has been able to surpass a maximum-likelihood baseline and a preference-based approach, and perform competitively against two contemporary large language models in all evaluation metrics. These findings substantiate the effectiveness of task-specific differentiable objectives for nuanced, controlled text-generation tasks.

</details>


### [51] [When Reasoning Hurts: Source-Aware Evaluation of Frontier LLMs for Clinical SOAP Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24902)
*Faizan Faisal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究推理增强型LLM在临床SOAP笔记生成任务中的表现，发现推理能力反而降低了GPT-5.4的性能，而DeepSeek-V4-Flash在推理模式下表现最佳；同源RAG带来模型依赖的小幅提升，表明强推理能力不应假设能自动提升临床文档生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理增强型LLM在医学推理基准上表现强劲，但尚不清楚这些优势能否迁移到结构化临床文档（如SOAP笔记）生成任务中。

Method: 使用SOAP笔记生成任务，基于OMI Health、ACI-Bench、PriMock57三个数据集构建源感知基准；评估GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Gemma-4-E4B三个模型，采用2x2实验设计独立控制提供者原生推理和同源检索增强生成（RAG）；使用七项自动指标和两个参考感知LLM裁判进行评估。

Result: 非推理模式的GPT-5.4在所有模型中整体质量最高；DeepSeek-V4-Flash在推理模式中最佳；启用推理显著降低了GPT-5.4在所有三个数据集上的性能；同源RAG带来较小且模型依赖的提升。

Conclusion: 强推理能力不应被假设能自动提升对保真度敏感的SOAP笔记生成任务，需进行专门的任务特定评估。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled LLMs perform strongly on medical reasoning benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains transfer to structured clinical documentation; we investigate this question using SOAP note generation from clinical dialogue in a source-aware benchmark spanning OMI Health, ACI-Bench, and PriMock57. We evaluate GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B in a controlled 2x2 design that independently toggles provider-native reasoning and same-source retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Outputs are assessed using seven automatic metrics alongside two reference-aware LLM judges. Both evaluation approaches agree that a non-reasoning GPT-5.4 configuration achieves the highest overall quality, while DeepSeek-V4-Flash performs best among reasoning-enabled configurations. Enabling reasoning significantly degrades GPT-5.4 performance across all three datasets, whereas same-source RAG yields smaller, model-dependent improvements. Overall, the findings indicate that stronger reasoning capability should not be assumed to improve fidelity-sensitive SOAP note generation without dedicated, task-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [Quantifying the Impact of Translation Errors on Multilingual LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24904)
*Klaudia-Doris Thellmann,Bernhard Stadler,Michael Färber,Jens Lehmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 机器翻译基准中的翻译错误会显著影响大语言模型的多语言评估准确性，且自动错误检测与人工标注的一致性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译基准广泛用于评估LLM的多语言能力，但基准中的翻译错误未得到充分研究，影响了多语言评估的可靠性和可比性。

Method: 使用LLM裁判的自动MQM风格错误跨度检测和跨度感知的QE基线（xCOMET-XXL），与专家人工标注进行比较，并分析翻译错误对基准准确率下降的影响。

Result: 跨度一致性在自然发生的基准翻译中具有非平凡性；目标端翻译错误与可测量的准确率百分点下降显著相关，即使在控制英文正确性和源端异常后仍然成立。

Conclusion: 翻译错误是导致多语言评估准确率下降的重要因素，自动错误检测与人工标注的一致性仍需改进。

Abstract: Machine-translated benchmarks are widely used to assess the multilingual capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet translation errors in these benchmarks remain underexplored, raising concerns about the reliability and comparability of multilingual evaluation. We address two practical gaps: (i) how well automatic MQM-style error spans from LLM judges and a span-aware QE baseline (xCOMET-XXL) match expert human span annotations on benchmark translations, and (ii) how strongly translation errors (as opposed to source-side issues in the English original) explain accuracy drops on translated benchmarks. We find that span agreement is non-trivial on naturally occurring benchmark translations, and that target-side translation errors are consistently associated with measurable, percentage-point drops in translated accuracy even after controlling for English correctness and source-side anomalies.

</details>


### [53] [Overview of the PsyDefDetect Shared Task at BioNLP 2026: Detecting Levels of Psychological Defense Mechanisms in Supportive Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24907)
*Hongbin Na,Zimu Wang,Zhaoming Chen,Yining Hua,Rena Gao,Kailai Yang,Ling Chen,Wei Wang,Shaoxiong Ji,John Torous,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyDefDetect是BioNLP@ACL 2026上关于检测情感支持对话中心理防御机制级别的共享任务，基于DMRS框架将求助者话语分类为9个类别。任务使用新发布的PsyDefConv语料库（200个对话，2336条标注话语），吸引172名参与者在CodaBench上提交563份结果，最优系统宏F1为0.420，仍留有提升空间。分析揭示了过预测多数类、准确率与宏F1差距扩大以及理论感知方法和LLM方法的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将临床心理学中经过验证的心理防御机制评级量表（DMRS）框架引入NLP，推动情感支持对话中细粒度防御功能分类这一临床心理学与NLP的新交叉领域研究。

Method: 基于DMRS框架，将目标求助者话语（结合前序对话上下文）分类为9个类别（7个层次DMRS级别+2个辅助标签）；使用新标注的PsyDefConv语料库；通过CodaBench平台组织竞赛。

Result: 最优系统宏F1为0.420，显著超过数据集论文中最强的微调基线，但仍有明显提升空间；分析发现三大问题：过预测多数类High-Adaptive、准确率与宏F1差距扩大（类别不平衡敏感性）、理论感知和LLM方法更具优势。

Conclusion: 任务展示了临床心理学与NLP交叉领域的可行性，现有最佳系统仍留有较大提升余地，发布所有任务材料以鼓励社区继续探索这一方向。

Abstract: We present an overview of PsyDefDetect, the shared task on detecting levels of psychological defense mechanisms in emotional support dialogues, co-located with BioNLP@ACL 2026. Grounded in the clinically validated Defense Mechanism Rating Scales (DMRS) framework, the task asks systems to classify a target seeker utterance, given its preceding dialogue context, into one of nine categories: seven hierarchical DMRS levels plus two auxiliary labels. Participants worked on PsyDefConv, a newly released corpus of 200 dialogues and 2336 help-seeker utterances annotated under DMRS with substantial inter-annotator agreement. The task attracted 172 participants on CodaBench who produced 563 submissions, with 21 teams officially registering their results for the final ranking. The best system achieved a macro F1-score of 0.420, surpassing the strongest fine-tuned baseline reported in the dataset paper by a notable margin, yet leaving clear headroom. Our analysis highlights (i) a persistent tendency to over-predict the majority High-Adaptive class, (ii) a widening gap between accuracy and macro-F1 that reveals class-imbalance sensitivity, and (iii) the value of theory-aware and LLM-based approaches for fine-grained defensive-function classification. We release all task materials and invite the community to continue work on this novel intersection of clinical psychology and NLP.

</details>


### [54] [MultiHaluDet: Multilingual Hallucination Detection via LLM Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24919)
*Riasad Alvi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MultiHaluDet，一种通过探测冻结LLM的完整隐藏状态轨迹来检测多语言幻觉的三阶段堆叠框架，无需语言特定微调，在英语基准上达到98.55% AUROC，并能跨高、中、低资源语言泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM幻觉是其可靠部署的关键障碍，尤其在非英语和资源受限环境中更为严重。现有依赖输出置信度或单层内部表示的检测方法，难以捕捉跨语言的深层复杂事实不一致性。

Method: 提出MultiHaluDet三阶段堆叠框架：1) 提取冻结LLM多层隐藏状态序列特征；2) 通过多尺度注意力和自注意力池化的混合架构处理；3) 生成out-of-fold嵌入输入校准的经典分类器集成。无需语言特定微调。

Result: 在Mistral-7B和LLaMA2-7B上，英文HaluEval和TriviaQA基准达98.55% AUROC，SOTA性能。跨法语（高资源）、孟加拉语（中资源）、阿姆哈拉语（低资源）均优于基线，展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: MultiHaluDet通过探测完整隐藏状态轨迹成功实现多语言幻觉检测的跨语言迁移，在不同语言层级上均表现出卓越的表示鲁棒性，解决了多语言幻觉检测的关键挑战。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) represent a critical barrier to their reliable deployment, a vulnerability heavily exacerbated in non-English and resource-constrained contexts. Existing detection approaches that rely on output confidence heuristics or single-layer internal representations frequently fail to capture deep, complex factual inconsistencies across diverse languages. To address this, we introduce MultiHaluDet, a novel three-stage stacking framework that detects multilingual hallucinations by probing the full hidden state trajectories of frozen LLMs without requiring language-specific fine-tuning. Our method extracts sequential features across multiple layers and processes them via a hybrid architecture using multi-scale attention and self-attention pooling. By generating out-of-fold embeddings that feed into a calibrated classical classifier ensemble, MultiHaluDet captures both fine-grained and coarse-grained patterns of factual inconsistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, reaching up to 98.55% AUROC on the English HaluEval and TriviaQA benchmarks using Mistral-7B and LLaMA2-7B architectures. Crucially, we rigorously evaluate our framework's cross-lingual generalization across high (French), medium (Bangla), and low-resource (Amharic) languages. MultiHaluDet demonstrates exceptional representational robustness, consistently outperforming baselines and successfully transferring hallucination detection capabilities across typologically diverse linguistic tiers.

</details>


### [55] [H$^{2}$MT: Semantic Hierarchy-Aware Hierarchical Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24930)
*Maryam Haghifam,Zifan He,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: H²MT通过构建语义层次结构并在推理时进行粗到细的路由，实现了长上下文的高效结构化推理，在多个基准上达到了更优的质量-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based LLMs在处理长输入时面临上下文窗口有限、预填充延迟和内存消耗随提示长度快速增长的问题。平坦的token流处理和基于分块的检索方法会在与查询无关的文本上花费大量计算和上下文预算。离线索引的RAG引入了外部存储和索引管理开销，且通常将检索证据作为原始文本追加，增加了预填充成本和延迟。

Method: H²MT构建了一个语义层次结构，通过自底向上的后序聚合为每个节点计算记忆嵌入，在推理时采用粗到细的路由策略，早期剪枝不相关的分支。

Result: 在LongBench QA（NarrativeQA, HotpotQA, QASPER）以及两个结构化技术文档设置上，H²MT相比提示压缩、记忆token方法和检索增强生成基线，实现了更低的峰值GPU内存和首Token延迟（TTFT），同时保持了具有竞争力的ROUGE-L和F1分数。

Conclusion: H²MT通过结构感知的长上下文推理方法，在质量和效率之间取得了有利的平衡，证明了层次化语义结构能够有效降低计算和内存开销而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based LLMs achieve strong results on many language tasks; however, long inputs remain challenging because context windows are finite, and prefill latency and memory grow rapidly with prompt length. Flat token-stream processing and chunk-based retrieval can therefore spend substantial computation and context budget on text unrelated to the query. Offline-indexed RAG additionally introduces external storage and index management overhead, and typically appends retrieved evidence as raw text, increasing prefill cost and latency. H^{2}MT makes long-context inference structure-aware: it builds a semantic hierarchy offline, computes a memory embedding for each node via bottom-up post-order aggregation, and routes queries coarse-to-fine at inference to prune irrelevant branches early. On LongBench QA (NarrativeQA, HotpotQA, QASPER) and two structured technical-document settings, H MT achieves favorable quality efficiency trade-offs, delivering competitive ROUGE-L and F1 (where applicable) with lower peak GPU memory and time-to-first-token (TTFT) than prompt compression, memory-token methods, and retrieval-augmented generation baselines.

</details>


### [56] [NITP: Next Implicit Token Prediction for LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24956)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Debing Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)，通过在表示空间中添加密集连续监督来增强标准next-token prediction，缓解隐状态退化和各向异性问题，在0.5B至9B的稠密和MoE模型上持续提升下游性能且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准next-token prediction (NTP)仅通过输出logit空间的离散标签监督语言模型，这种稀疏的one-hot监督使得潜在表示空间约束不足，导致隐状态可能漂移到退化和各向异性的配置，限制泛化能力。

Method: 提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)，在离散预测之外，通过在表示空间中添加密集连续监督来增强训练。具体地，训练模型预测下一个token的隐式语义内容，使用同一模型浅层表示作为稳定的自监督目标。理论分析表明NITP通过缓解欠约束自由度并鼓励紧凑、结构化的表示几何来正则化优化景观。

Result: 在0.5B至9B参数的稠密和MoE模型上，NITP持续提升下游性能：在9B MoE模型上，MMLU-Pro绝对提升5.7%，C3提升6.4%，CommonsenseQA提升4.3%，仅增加约2%的训练FLOPs且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: NITP通过表示空间的密集连续监督有效正则化了语言模型的表示几何，以极小的计算开销显著提升了下游任务性能。

Abstract: Standard next-token prediction (NTP) supervises language models solely through discrete labels in the output logit space. We argue that this sparse one-hot supervision leaves the latent representation space under-constrained, allowing hidden states to drift into degenerate and anisotropic configurations that can limit generalization. To address this issue, we propose Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP), which augments discrete prediction with dense continuous supervision directly in the representation space. NITP trains the model to predict the implicit semantic content of the next token, using shallow-layer representations from the same model as stable self-supervised targets. We provide theoretical analysis showing that NITP regularizes the optimization landscape by mitigating under-constrained degrees of freedom and encouraging a compact, structured representation geometry. Empirically, across dense and MoE models ranging from 0.5B to 9B parameters, NITP consistently improves downstream performance with negligible computational overhead. On a 9B MoE model, NITP achieves a 5.7% absolute improvement on MMLU-Pro, along with gains of 6.4% on C3 and 4.3% on CommonsenseQA, with approximately 2% additional training FLOPs and no additional inference cost. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/aHapBean/NITP.

</details>


### [57] [Investigating the Interplay between Contextual and Parametric Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness under Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24960)
*Jingyi Sun,Qianli Wang,Pepa Atanasova,Nils Feldhus,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一偏好对齐接口FaithMate，研究CoT忠实性中上下文忠实性与参数忠实性两种范式的相互关系。发现两范式正相关但不对称：优化参数忠实性可提升两范式表现，而上下文忠实性提升效果不稳定；上下文忠实性不同指标间存在内在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实性评估分为上下文忠实性（扰动输入或CoT轨迹）和参数忠实性（干预模型参数知识）两种范式，但二者仅被描述性比较，缺乏定量、统一的对比研究，且对两种范式之间是否存在相互影响及如何泛化尚不清楚。

Method: 提出FaithMate——一个统一的偏好对齐接口，可针对任一种忠实性范式优化模型。通过在三个模型、两个数据集和六种忠实性指标上进行实验，系统考察两种范式之间的增益泛化情况。

Result: 两种范式正耦合但不对称：优化参数忠实性可同时提升两种范式表现；优化上下文忠实性带来的增益不稳定。上下文范式内部，不同忠实性指标间的增益不相互迁移，表明现有上下文指标衡量的是忠实性的不同方面，存在内在权衡。

Conclusion: CoT忠实性不是一个单一目标，需要多面优化和评估，不能仅依赖单一忠实性范式或指标。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness, i.e., whether CoTs genuinely reflect large language models' (LLM) underlying behavior, is typically evaluated under two disjoint paradigms: contextual faithfulness, measured by perturbing the input or CoT trace, and parametric faithfulness, assessed by intervening on a model's parametric knowledge. Yet prior work compares them only descriptively. We fill this gap by proposing FaithMate, a unified preference-alignment interface for optimizing models towards either faithfulness paradigm. It enables us to investigate the interplay between the two paradigms, examining whether and to what extent faithfulness gains generalize within and across paradigms. Across three models, two datasets, and six faithfulness metrics, we find that the two paradigms are positively coupled, yet asymmetric: optimizing towards parametric faithfulness yields consistent gains across both paradigms, whereas the contextual counterpart delivers more variable gains. Within the contextual paradigm, faithfulness gains on one metric do not consistently transfer to others, implying that existing contextual metrics capture disjoint facets of faithfulness and exposing inherent trade-offs. These findings imply that CoT faithfulness is not a monolithic objective and therefore requires multifaceted optimization and evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Large Language Model Selection with Limited Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24981)
*Yavuz Durmazkeser,Patrik Okanovic,Andreas Kirsch,Torsten Hoefler,Nezihe Merve Gürel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SELECT-LLM是一个无需访问模型权重或架构信息、基于期望信息增益的主动模型选择框架，能够在少量标注下高效识别最优大语言模型，在23个数据集、156个模型上实现高达84.78%的标注成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估依赖在固定评估集上进行昂贵的人工标注，计算成本高且效率低；面对众多候选模型，需要一种能主动选择最具信息量的查询来识别最优模型的方法，以大幅降低评估成本。

Method: 提出SELECT-LLM框架，核心是基于期望信息增益（Expected Information Gain）的查询选择规则，该规则通过计算候选模型输出之间的成对相似性来量化每条查询的信息价值；仅依赖模型生成响应，无需假设模型架构或访问权重，因此适用于开源和黑盒LLM。

Result: 在23个数据集、156个评估模型、多种任务族和文本评估指标上，SELECT-LLM在所有设置中均优于最强基线，最优模型选择的标注成本降低高达81.8%，近最优模型选择降低高达84.78%。

Conclusion: SELECT-LLM是首个面向大语言模型的主动模型选择框架，通过基于信息增益的查询选择策略，能够在不依赖模型内部信息的情况下，以极低的标注成本高效识别最优或近最优模型。

Abstract: Choosing a Large Language Model (LLM) for a given task requires comparing many strong candidates, yet standard evaluation relies on costly annotations over fixed evaluation sets. To address this challenge, we develop SELECT-LLM, the first framework for active model selection of LLMs. SELECT-LLM aims to find a small set of queries whose annotations are most informative for identifying the best LLM for a given task. To this end, we introduce a query selection rule based on expected information gain, computed from pairwise similarities between candidate model outputs. Because this rule only uses generated model responses, SELECT-LLM can be applied across candidate models without assumptions about their architecture or access to model weights. This makes it suitable for both open-weight and black-box LLMs. We evaluate SELECT-LLM across 23 datasets, 156 evaluated models, diverse task families, and multiple text evaluation metrics. Across all experiments, SELECT-LLM improves over the strongest baseline in every setting, with annotation cost reductions up to 81.8% for best model selection and up to 84.78% for near-best model selection.

</details>


### [59] [Exploring Profiles of Cognitive Distortions Associated with Mental Health Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24996)
*Alina Anikejeva,Kairit Sirts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用n-gram和微调transformer模型，分析了Reddit上九个自我报告的心理健康群体和一个对照组的认知扭曲模式，发现所有心理健康群体（无论是综合还是单独）的认知扭曲 prevalence 均高于对照组，效应量从小到中等；不同心理健康状况之间的扭曲轮廓大致相似，但某些群体整体扭曲水平更高。结果表明，简单的词汇方法可用于大规模心理健康文本数据的群体趋势探索性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管认知扭曲与多种心理健康障碍相关，但现有研究主要聚焦于抑郁症。本研究旨在探索多种心理健康状况下的认知扭曲轮廓，填补跨心理健康条件比较研究的空白。

Method: 使用基于n-gram的方法和微调transformer模型，分析包含九个自我报告心理健康群体和对照组的Reddit数据集，检测认知扭曲。

Result: 心理健康群体（综合和单独）的认知扭曲 prevalence 均高于对照组，效应量从小到中等；不同状况间的扭曲轮廓大致相似，但某些群体整体扭曲水平更高。

Conclusion: 简单的词汇方法（如n-gram）可用于大规模心理健康文本数据中群体层面趋势的探索性分析。

Abstract: Cognitive distortions, distorted patterns of thinking, have been increasingly studied in computational mental health research. Although they are related to many, if not all, mental health disorders, most existing studies focus primarily on depression. In this work, we explore distortion profiles across multiple mental health conditions. We analyzed a large Reddit-based dataset containing posts from nine self-reported mental health groups as well as a control group using both an n-gram-based method and a fine-tuned transformer model for detecting cognitive distortions. Mental health groups, both when pooled together and when examined individually, showed higher prevalence of cognitive distortions compared to the control group, with the effect sizes ranging from small to moderate. When comparing distortion profiles across conditions, we observed largely similar patterns, although some groups exhibited overall higher levels of distortions than others. These findings suggest that relatively simple lexical approaches can be useful for exploratory analyses of group-level trends in large-scale mental health text data.

</details>


### [60] [Better, Faster: Harnessing Self-Improvement in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24998)
*Qihuang Zhong,Liang Ding,Juhua Liu,Bo Du,Leszek Rutkowski,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大型推理模型自我提升训练中存在的数据不平衡与过度思考问题，提出HSIR方法，通过验证后退出采样策略缓解数据不平衡，并设计内在多样性分数筛选冗余推理样本，结合H-GRPO强化学习算法，在提升推理性能的同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型虽可通过自我生成推理轨迹进行自我提升训练，但在复杂推理任务中常表现不佳甚至出现模型崩溃。初步分析揭示了两大问题：训练数据不平衡（简单样本多而关键困难样本稀缺）以及过度思考（包含大量冗余推理步骤的不良样本被用于自我训练），亟需有效方法解决这些问题。

Method: 提出HSIR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 验证后退出采样策略（verify-then-exit sampling），高效收集困难查询的准确解，缓解数据不平衡；(2) 内在多样性分数（Intrinsic Diversity score），量化过度思考程度并过滤冗余解。此外，进一步提出H-GRPO算法，将内在多样性作为外部奖励，通过强化学习鼓励简洁且多样化的推理。

Result: HSIR在多种后训练范式上均取得了显著效果，平均性能提升最高达+10.9%，同时相对推理开销降低最高达42.4%。

Conclusion: HSIR有效解决了大型推理模型自我提升中的关键问题，不仅显著增强了推理性能，还大幅提升了推理效率，为复杂推理任务中的自我训练提供了可行的优化方向。

Abstract: Self-improvement training enables the large reasoning models (LRMs) to improve themselves by self-generating reasoning trajectories as training data without external supervision. However, we find that this method often falls short in complex reasoning tasks and even leads to model collapse. Through a series of preliminary analyses, we reveal two problems: (1) data imbalance, where most training samples are simple, but the challenging yet crucial samples are scarce; (2) overthinking, where many undesired samples with redundant reasoning steps are used for self-training. To this end, we propose HSIR, which effectively Harnesses Self-Improvement in large Reasoning models via two simple-yet-effective approaches. Specifically, HSIR introduces a verify-then-exit sampling strategy to mitigate data imbalance by efficiently collecting more accurate solutions for difficult queries, and designs an Intrinsic Diversity score to quantify overthinking and filter out the undesired solutions. We apply HSIR to various post-training paradigms, among which we further propose H-GRPO, an enhanced GRPO algorithm that leverages the intrinsic diversity as an external reward to encourage concise and diverse reasoning via reinforcement learning. Extensive results show that HSIR not only effectively enhances the reasoning performance, i.e., bringing up to +10.9% average performance gains, but also significantly improves the reasoning efficiency by reducing up to 42.4% relative inference overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [61] [Algometrics: Forecasting Under Algorithmic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23978)
*Marc Schmitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法计量学"(algometrics)框架，研究预测模型在算法市场中改变自身所预测数据生成过程的问题，证明被动历史数据无法识别部署风险，历史模型排名可能在拥挤效应下反转，并给出部署风险估计的有限样本界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法市场中预测模型通过影响交易、配置、执行或风控决策，反过来改变其预测所依据的未来数据分布，形成反馈循环。传统的时序建模方法忽略这一因果闭环，导致模型评估与实际部署效果严重偏离。

Method: 建立algometrics理论框架，区分"历史风险"(被动预测下测量)与"部署风险"(预测驱动行动时测量)。通过线性反馈模型给出三个理论结果：(1)非可识别性证明；(2)模型排名反转；(3)随机化/工具变量方法结合有限样本界估计部署风险。

Result: (1)仅凭被动历史数据无法识别部署风险，存在无穷多算法中介环境共享同一历史律但部署风险不同；(2)拥挤效应下历史模型排名可能完全反转；(3)随机化或工具变量行动可识别短视距线性反馈，并给出了部署风险估计的有限样本上界。

Conclusion: 算法市场的时序基准应当在报告预测精度的同时报告反馈敏感性，以反映模型在影响数据生成后的实际表现。

Abstract: In algorithmic markets, predictive models become part of the data-generating process they aim to forecast. Once their outputs are converted into trades, allocations, execution schedules, or risk controls, they change the future data on which they are evaluated. I introduce algometrics, a framework for time series whose evolution depends on the predictive algorithms forecasting them. The framework distinguishes historical risk, measured under passive forecasting, from deployment risk, measured when forecasts drive actions. I prove three results. First, deployment risk is not identifiable from passive historical data alone: even in a one-step linear feedback model, infinitely many algorithm-mediated environments induce the same historical law while implying different deployment risks for the same forecaster. Second, historical model rankings can invert under crowding, so a predictor with lower passive error can have higher deployment error once similar algorithms are adopted. Third, randomized or instrumented actions identify short-horizon linear feedback, and I derive a finite-sample bound for deployment-risk estimation. These results suggest that time-series benchmarks in algorithmic markets should report feedback sensitivity alongside predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [62] [Iterative Refinement Neural Operators are Learned Fixed-Point Solvers: A Principled Approach to Spectral Bias Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24041)
*Xiaotian Liu,Shuyuan Shang,Xiaopeng Wang,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代细化神经算子（IRNO），通过固定点迭代的逐步残差校正机制增强预训练算子，结合渐进式频谱损失函数，有效解决了神经算子在建模高频细节时的频谱偏差问题。在湍流和活性物质等物理系统上，IRNO显著降低了预测误差，高频误差比降至1.48-2.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子采用单次前向推理过程，难以捕捉高频细节（频谱偏差），限制了其在多尺度物理建模中的精度。

Method: IRNO在预训练算子基础上增加一个可学习的细化模块，通过固定点迭代进行残差校正；并设计渐进式频谱损失，在训练过程中逐步增加高频分量的惩罚权重。

Result: 湍流模拟误差降低高达56.05%；在活性物质系统中，低、中、高频归一化误差比分别降至27.72-36.10%、5.07-6.68%和1.48-2.04%，且超出训练迭代次数后仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: IRNO通过迭代细化策略有效缓解了频谱偏差，兼具收敛理论保证和实际性能提升，拓展了神经算子在高精度科学计算中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Neural operators serve as fast, data-driven surrogates for scientific modeling but typically rely on a monolithic, single-pass inference procedure that struggles to resolve high-frequency details, a limitation known as spectral bias. We introduce the Iterative Refinement Neural Operator (IRNO), which augments pre-trained operators with a learned refinement module iteratively applied via fixed-point iteration. IRNO decomposes the prediction into a coarse initialization followed by successive residual corrections, paralleling classical numerical solvers. Under local assumptions, we establish contraction of the induced operator, ensuring convergence to a unique fixed point. To explicitly target high-frequency errors, we propose a progressive spectral loss that adaptively increases penalty on high-frequency components over refinement steps during training. Across physical systems, IRNO consistently lowers error, with up to 56.05% improvement on turbulent flow. On Active Matter, spectral analysis reveals that, relative to base operator, the normalized error ratios decrease to 27.72-36.10% in low-, 5.07-6.68% in mid-, and 1.48-2.04% in high-frequencies, remaining stable beyond the trained iteration count. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator

</details>


### [63] [Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042)
*Alexander Okezue Bell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了单层隐藏状态的高斯发布机制在隐私与效用之间的权衡。实验表明，在1536种高斯发布协方差中，没有一种能在自适应检索攻击下同时实现中等效用和中等隐私。理论证明了Fisher球下界：任何具有O(1) Fisher效用的满秩高斯发布都存在方向使得马氏信号随隐藏宽度线性增长，排除了高斯类中的均匀安全性，与实证的"空中间"现象一致。对角逆Fisher发布Σ*_diag(K) = (2K/d) diag(1/F_ii)是首个在KL预算K下唯一最小最大最优的对角机制，且在32层模型网格上每个点的最差攻击者top-1≤0.001，但它位于隐私/效用边缘而非填充中间。广义特征机制在欧氏检索下达到13倍帕累托改进，但在自适应马氏攻击下崩溃到100% top-1；全轨迹序列逆变器在干净GPT-2前缀上恢复94%，但在Σ_diag下为0%。从头训练的分离记忆Transformer在90M参数时达到G_Mah∈[20,33]，且在30M到1B参数范围内比同预算GPT基线保持6-24倍优势，固定令牌语言建模损失略有惩罚；预训练模型最高仅达9.3。这些结果将隐藏状态发布从高斯类内的机制设计重新定位为架构或发布协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私发布机制在高维隐藏状态场景下存在严重的隐私-效用权衡问题，缺乏既能保护隐私又保持实用性的高斯发布机制。作者旨在揭示高斯类发布的根本局限性，并探索超越纯机制设计的解决方案。

Method: 采用理论推导（Fisher球下界）与实证评估相结合的方法：测试1536种高斯发布协方差、比较对角逆Fisher机制与广义特征机制、设计分离记忆Transformer架构，并在30M-1B参数范围内与GPT基线进行对比。

Result: 1) 无高斯发布能同时实现中等隐私和效用；2) 对角逆Fisher是最优对角机制但处于边缘；3) 广义特征机制在自适应攻击下完全失效；4) 分离记忆Transformer在90M参数达到G_Mah∈[20,33]，比GPT基线有6-24倍优势，但预训练模型仅达9.3。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态发布问题的核心应从高斯类内的机制设计转向架构与发布的协同设计，因为纯机制设计在高维隐藏状态场景下存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Of $1{,}536$ Gaussian release covariances we tested for single-layer hidden-state privacy, zero achieve both moderate utility and moderate privacy against an adaptive retrieval attacker. We prove a complementary Fisher-ball lower bound: every full-rank Gaussian release at $O(1)$ Fisher utility admits a direction whose Mahalanobis signal grows linearly in hidden width, ruling out uniform Gaussian safety in the class and matching the empirical empty middle. The diagonal inverse-Fisher release $Σ^\star_{\mathrm{diag}}(\mathcal{K}) = (2\mathcal{K}/d)\,\mathrm{diag}(1/F_{ii})$ is the unique minimax-optimal diagonal mechanism at first-order KL budget $\mathcal{K}$ and the only release with worst-attacker top-1 $\le 0.001$ at every point of a 32 model-layer grid, but it sits on a privacy/utility edge rather than filling the middle. A generalized-eigen mechanism reaching $13\times$ Pareto reduction under Euclidean retrieval collapses to $100\%$ top-1 under the adaptive Mahalanobis attacker, and a full-trajectory sequence inverter recovers $94\%$ of clean GPT-2 prefixes but $0\%$ under $Σ_{\mathrm{diag}}$. A split-memory transformer trained from scratch reaches $G_{\mathrm{Mah}} \in [20, 33]$ at 90M and maintains a $6$--$24\times$ advantage over same-budget GPT baselines from 30M to 1B at a fixed-token language-modeling loss penalty; pretrained models top out at 9.3. These results reframe hidden-state release from mechanism-design within the Gaussian class to architecture or release co-design.

</details>


### [64] [A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark: Do Protein-Ligand Models Learn Binding Sites or Just Binding Likelihood?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24045)
*Zhaohan Meng,Zhen Bai,Ke Yuan,Iadh Ounis,Zaiqiao Meng,Hao Xu,Joseph Loscalzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了InteractBind，一个包含约10万蛋白质-配体对的大规模数据集和基准，用于细粒度评估蛋白质-配体相互作用，包括结合位点定位和六种非共价相互作用的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质-配体基准仅评估是否结合及结合强度（如二元结合预测和亲和力回归），缺乏对模型能否定位结合位点或识别分子识别中非共价相互作用的评估。

Method: 构建了包含约10万蛋白质-配体对的InteractBind数据集，以结合位点定位为核心细粒度任务，使用蛋白质残基和配体原子间六种非共价相互作用的交互图来评估模型的结合位点定位能力；同时包含结合亲和力和蛋白质相似性控制的数据划分以支持泛化评估。

Result: 评估了8种现有的基于序列和基于交互的模型，发现模型在二元结合预测任务上表现良好，但结合位点定位能力有限，且在不同非共价相互作用类型上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: InteractBind建立了一种鼓励开发更具可解释性和物理基础的蛋白质-配体模型的基准范式。

Abstract: Protein-ligand modeling underpins computational drug discovery and molecular design. Existing protein-ligand benchmarks typically evaluate whether a protein and ligand interact and how strongly they bind, through tasks such as binary binding prediction and affinity regression. However, these evaluations provide limited evidence of whether models can localize binding sites or identify the non-covalent interactions underlying molecular recognition. To address this gap, we introduce InteractBind, a large-scale protein-ligand dataset comprising approximately 100k protein-ligand pairs, together with a benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. The core fine-grained task is that of binding-site localization, which uses protein-residue and ligand-atom interaction maps spanning six major types of non-covalent interactions to assess whether model-derived interaction maps localize binding sites. InteractBind further includes binding affinity and protein similarity-controlled splits to support realistic generalization assessment. Using InteractBind, we evaluate eight existing sequence-based and interaction-aware models, assessing binary binding prediction and binding-site localization. Results reveal limited binding-site localization despite strong binary binding prediction, with marked variation across non-covalent interaction types. Overall, InteractBind establishes a benchmark paradigm that encourages the development of more interpretable and physically grounded protein-ligand models.

</details>


### [65] [Mixture of Complementary Agents for Robust LLM Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24048)
*Yichi Zhang,Kevin Lu,Yuang Zhang,Jie Gao,Lirong Xia,Fang-Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了多AI协作中的提议者选择问题，将其视为组合选择问题，提出基于互补性的贪心选择算法，在性能和成本之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注准确性或多样性，忽视了提议者之间以及与摘要器之间的交互作用，需要一种更有效的选择方法。

Method: 将提议者选择重新定义为类似于特征选择的组合选择问题，利用少量标注数据评估互补性，探索多种计算可行的贪心选择算法。

Result: 验证了互补性作为提议者选择的指导原则的有效性，识别出在实践中实现最佳性能-成本权衡的方法。

Conclusion: 基于互补性的贪心选择算法能够有效替代传统方法，在有限计算资源下实现更好的多AI协作效果。

Abstract: Multi-AI collaboration, such as ensembling or debating large language models (LLMs), is a promising paradigm for aggregating information and boosting performance. A foundational step in these pipelines is to feed the responses of several proposer LLMs into a summarizer LLM, which synthesizes a better answer. However, choosing which proposers to include is non-trivial. Existing approaches primarily focus either on accuracy (picking the strongest models) or diversity (ensuring variety), and often overlook the interactions among proposers and with the summarizer. We reframe proposer selection as a combinatorial selection problem akin to feature selection, where the value of an LLM lies in its complementarity with others. However, directly applying standard feature-selection algorithms is impractical in the LLM setting due to prohibitive time complexity. Motivated by this limitation, we explore an extensive range of computationally feasible, greedy-style selection algorithms that assess complementarity using a small labeled set. Our experiments validate complementarity as a guiding principle for proposer selection and identify methods that achieve the best performance-cost trade-offs in practice.

</details>


### [66] [Truthful Online Preference Aggregation for LLM Fine-Tuning in Mobile Crowdsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24052)
*Shugang Hao,Lingjie Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对移动群智感知中基于人类反馈的LLM微调问题，提出了一种在线加权聚合机制，通过动态调整工人权重来激励真实反馈，实现了次线性遗憾O(√T)，并在有限反馈场景下仍保持该性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动群智感知平台在利用LLM生成内容并收集工人反馈时，工人可能策略性地虚报偏好以最大化自身影响力或报酬。现有方法（如基于EM的权重估计）无法在线识别最准确的工人，导致线性遗憾O(T)。

Method: 建立动态贝叶斯博弈模型刻画平台与策略性工人之间的多智能体在线学习过程；提出在线加权聚合机制，根据工人反馈准确性动态调整其聚合权重；理论证明该机制能激励真实反馈并实现次线性遗憾。

Result: 理论证明所提机制能确保策略性工人提供真实反馈，并实现O(√T)的次线性遗憾；扩展至有限工人反馈场景仍保持O(√T)遗憾；在真实数据集上的LLM微调实验表明性能显著优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 本文为移动群智感知中LLM微调的在线偏好聚合提供了理论保障，有效解决了策略性工人虚报反馈的问题，并在理论和实验两方面验证了机制的有效性。

Abstract: To better serve users' demands in mobile applications (e.g., navigation), mobile crowdsourcing platforms can iteratively align large language model (LLM)-generated content (e.g., AI-generated traffic condition predictions) with human feedback collected from crowdsourcing workers (e.g., mobile users). However, workers may strategically misreport their online preference feedback to maximize their influence or payment. Existing pipelines in mobile crowdsourcing (e.g., EM-based weight estimation) fail to identify the most accurate worker in this online setting, resulting in a linear regret $\mathcal{O}(T)$ over $T$ time slots. In this paper, we study truthful online preference aggregation for LLM fine-tuning in mobile crowdsourcing. We formulate a new dynamic Bayesian game to model the multi-agent online learning process between the platform and strategic mobile workers. We propose a novel online weighted aggregation mechanism that dynamically adjusts each worker's weight in the preference aggregation according to their feedback accuracy. We prove that our mechanism ensures truthful feedback from strategic workers and achieves a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ over $T$ time slots. We further extend our mechanism to a challenging scenario with limited worker feedback per time slot, still guaranteeing a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$. Experiments on LLM fine-tuning with real-world datasets further demonstrate significant performance gains of our mechanisms over benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [67] [Cascade-KDE: Robust Time-Series Restoration under Out-of-Distribution Impulse Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24055)
*Yuefeng Liu,Ning Yang,Ziyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cascade-KDE，一种无需训练的时间序列修复框架，通过二维时空密度估计、密度截断鲁棒期望和指数级联自适应停止，在保留局部特征的同时有效去除高斯噪声和脉冲异常值，在多个基准数据集上优于经典滤波器和代表性学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列数据常被高斯噪声和大规模脉冲异常值污染，现有方法在保持导数峰值和任务关键特征方面表现不足，需要一种无需训练、能同时去除噪声并保留局部形状特征的修复方法。

Method: 1) 二维时间-幅度密度估计；2) 密度截断鲁棒期望，限制远距离异常点的影响；3) 指数级联自适应停止，逐步精化序列。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Cascade-KDE在曲线保真度、导数保持、下游分类和运行时效率方面均一致优于经典滤波器和代表性学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 有界密度修正是噪声时间序列预处理中一种实用的特征保留方案。

Abstract: Real-world time-series data in industrial sensing, healthcare, and energy systems is often corrupted by a mixture of Gaussian noise and occasional large-magnitude impulse outliers. For tasks that depend on local shape, such as ECG morphology analysis and battery degradation monitoring, the main requirement is not only low reconstruction error but also preservation of derivative peaks and task-critical features. We propose Cascade-KDE, a training-free restoration framework for corrupted time series. The method first estimates a two-dimensional temporal-amplitude density, then applies a Density-Truncated Robust Expectation to limit the influence of distant abnormal points, and finally refines the sequence through an exponential cascade with adaptive stopping. This design aims to improve robustness under out-of-distribution impulse corruptions while keeping the restored trajectory close to the original local structure. Across several benchmark datasets, the proposed method shows consistent gains over classical filters and representative learning-based baselines on curve fidelity, derivative preservation, downstream classification, and runtime efficiency. These results suggest that bounded density-based restoration is a practical option for feature-preserving preprocessing in noisy time-series pipelines.

</details>


### [68] [Signs Beat Floats: Low-Rank Double-Binary Adaptation for On-Device Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24058)
*Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Yuma Ichikawa,Yudai Fujimoto,Akira Sakai,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRDBA是一种兼容LoRA的适配器，将低秩因子替换为二进制符号载体并通过通道级缩放表示幅度，在保持性能的同时显著降低适配器存储开销（超过10倍），在适配器模式下匹配fp16 LoRA质量，预填充延迟开销不超过8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧部署大语言模型时，通常使用量化基模型加小型LoRA适配器的方式。然而在未合并的适配器模式下，LoRA适配器除了紧凑存储外，还会引入额外的密集浮点分支，带来存储、通信和计算开销。作者希望在保持LoRA兼容性的前提下，进一步压缩适配器规模，降低端侧部署的资源需求。

Method: LoRDBA将LoRA的两个低秩因子替换为二进制符号载体（仅包含+1/-1），并通过轻量级通道级缩放（channel-wise scales）来表示幅度信息。这使得密集的适配器分支转化为两次符号累加矩阵乘法与通道级缩放的交替操作，大幅降低了存储和计算开销。

Result: 在适配器模式下，LoRDBA在匹配模型大小时优于低位基线方法，在特定场景下匹配fp16 LoRA质量。在rank=16时，未合并适配器的预填充延迟开销最多8%，适配器存储足迹降低超过10倍，训练内存开销约为fp16 LoRA的1.6倍。

Conclusion: LoRDBA通过二值化低秩因子加通道级缩放的方案，在保持LoRA兼容性和竞争力的前提下，大幅降低了适配器的存储和通信开销，适合端侧部署和热切换场景。

Abstract: On-device adaptation of large language models commonly keeps a quantized base model frozen while training and deploying a small, task-specific LoRA adapter. In the unmerged adapter-mode setting, however, the adapter is more than a compact storage module; it introduces an additional dense floating-point branch, maintains a trainable state for local updates, and acts as a unit of communication and hot-swapping.We introduce LoRDBA, a LoRA-compatible adapter that replaces both low-rank factors with binary sign carriers while representing magnitudes through lightweight, channel-wise scales, converting the dense adapter branch into two sign-accumulation matrix multiplications interleaved with channel-wise scaling. A finite-sample analysis shows that reconstruction quality is governed by the residual-to-magnitude ratio of the original LoRA factors. In adapter-mode experiments, LoRDBA outperforms low-bit baselines at matched model sizes while matching fp16 LoRA quality in selected regimes. The unmerged adapter incurs at most 8% prefill latency overhead at matched rank r=16 despite an over 10x reduction in adapter footprint, with moderate training memory overhead of approximately 1.6x that of fp16 LoRA.

</details>


### [69] [Spectral Probe-Circuits: A Three-Step Recipe for Identifying Attention-Head Circuits in Pretrained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24059)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种三步法来识别预训练Transformer中的注意力头电路：基于谱信号的头部排名、任务模式筛选、组消融验证。在多个模型规模、架构和预训练流程上验证，发现2-6个头的归纳电路是因果必需的，且谱信号无需监督即可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖标注或归因梯度来识别注意力头电路，缺乏一种通用、无监督且可跨模型迁移的电路识别方法。

Method: 三步法：(1) 计算每个注意力头输出的时间积分参与比作为谱信号，对头部进行排序；(2) 通过任务模式筛选得到特定任务的候选电路；(3) 对候选电路进行组消融，与随机对照比较完成因果验证。

Result: 在51M-1B激活/7B总参数、密集和MoE架构、四种预训练流程上验证。2-6个头的归纳电路消融后导致94-100%的性能下降。谱信号在无监督下能预测不同种子的特定电路。17-19%的头部进行可识别的专门化计算，归纳电路规模为3-11个头，与总头数呈次线性关系。

Conclusion: 该方法是三篇论文系列的方法论锚点，提供了跨模型规模、架构和预训练流程通用的注意力头电路识别框架，揭示了电路结构的跨模型守恒性。

Abstract: We present a three-step recipe for identifying attention-head circuits in pretrained transformers. A per-head spectral signal -- the time-integrated participation ratio of each head's attention output -- ranks heads doing sustained content-dependent computation without labels or attribution gradients. A task-pattern screen filters this general indicator into a task-specific candidate circuit, and group ablation against a matched-random control completes the causal claim. We validate across an 8x parameter range (51M to 1B-active / 7B-total), two architecture families (dense, mixture-of-experts), and four pretraining pipelines. The recipe ports: a 2-6 head induction circuit is causally necessary in every model tested, with a 94-100% drop in synthetic-induction top-1 after ablation. The spectral signal is predictive without supervision: on six independent seeds of a 51M-parameter probe model, the same computation identifies the seed-specific circuit on each seed. The fraction of heads doing identifiable specialized computation is conserved at 17-19% across the Pythia family (124M to 410M), while specific induction circuits stay 3-11 heads -- sublinear in total head count. This paper is the methodology anchor of a three-paper program; companion papers extend the recipe to developmental trajectories during pretraining and to composed-task circuits where pattern selectivity decouples from task-causal structure.

</details>


### [70] [Federated Learning over Human-Body Communication for On-Body Edge Intelligence: A Survey, Taxonomy, and BODYFED-HBC Scheduling Vignette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24062)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了人体通信（HBC）与联邦学习（FL）在可穿戴体域网中的交叉领域，提出了区分体内、体中心、跨用户和临床云FL部署的分类法，并定义了体信道感知FL这一开放问题。文章介绍了BODYFED-HBC参考架构、优化公式与调度算法，以及结合可穿戴数据集与人体耦合通信信号损耗模型的仿真方案，最后指出了开放数据集、评估指标和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HBC和FL两个研究领域关联薄弱：穿戴式FL通常抽象掉通信层，而HBC研究通常忽略学习和模型更新流量。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，推动体信道感知的联邦学习研究。

Method: 通过综述性调查，提出HBC与FL交叉的分类法（区分四种部署场景），定义体信道感知FL问题，设计BODYFED-HBC参考架构，给出优化公式和调度算法，并构建结合公开数据集与经验性人体耦合通信信号损耗模型的可复现仿真框架。

Result: 提出了体信道感知FL的概念，即客户端选择、更新压缩和聚合过程由依赖姿态的HBC链路、剩余能量、传感器内存和隐私风险共同控制的协议；提供了BODYFED-HBC架构及配套优化公式与调度算法；给出了结合真实数据集与信道模型的仿真方案。

Conclusion: 本文为计算机科学研究者提供了HBC与FL交叉领域的系统综述、分类框架、参考架构和仿真工具，明确了开放数据集、评估指标和未来研究方向，推动了体域网中通信与学习联合优化的研究议程。

Abstract: Human-body communication (HBC) is a promising physical substrate for wearable body-area networks because it can localize communication around the body and reduce the burden of conventional radio links. Federated learning (FL) is a promising learning substrate because it can reduce raw-data centralization for physiological and behavioral sensing. Yet these two literatures remain weakly connected: FL for wearables usually abstracts the communication layer, whereas HBC research usually abstracts learning and model-update traffic. This article surveys the intersection of HBC, wireless body-area networks, wearable FL, Internet-of-Bodies privacy, and edge-intelligence optimization. We propose a taxonomy that distinguishes intra-body, body-hub, cross-user, and clinical-cloud FL deployments, and we identify the open problem of body-channel-aware FL: learning protocols whose client selection, update compression, and aggregation are controlled by posture-dependent HBC links, residual energy, sensor memory, and privacy risk. To make the research agenda concrete, we introduce BODYFED-HBC as a reference architecture and provide an optimization formulation and scheduling algorithm. We further specify a reproducible simulation vignette that combines public wearable datasets with empirical body-coupled-communication signal-loss models. The article concludes with open datasets, evaluation metrics, limitations, and research directions for computer scientists working above the hardware layer.

</details>


### [71] [Generative Representation Learning on Hyper-relational Knowledge Graphs via Masked Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24064)
*Jaejun Lee,Seheon Kim,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超关系知识图谱中更一般化的事实生成任务（而非简单的链接预测），并设计了基于掩码离散扩散的生成式表示学习方法KREPE，统一了链接预测与事实生成，在标准基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超关系知识图谱推理方法局限于简单的链接预测，假设事实中几乎所有成分已知，仅留一个空白待填充。但现实场景中事实的多个甚至全部成分可能同时缺失，现有方法无法处理。

Method: 提出KREPE，首个用于超关系知识图谱的生成式表示学习方法。通过掩码离散扩散建模缺失成分的条件概率分布，利用上下文消息传递建模事实内部依赖，通过随机采样上下文聚合建模事实间关联。

Result: 在标准HKG链接预测基准上达到SOTA，并在生成新颖且正确的事实任务上优于基于大语言模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: KREPE将链接预测与事实生成统一在同一训练框架中，有效解决了超关系知识图谱中更一般化的事实生成问题。

Abstract: Hyper-relational knowledge graphs (HKGs) effectively represent complex facts. While inferring new knowledge in HKGs is a critical problem, current methods cast it as a simple link prediction, assuming that nearly all entities and relations within a fact are known, leaving only a single blank to be filled. However, this restricted assumption may not hold in real-world scenarios in which multiple, or even all, constituent components of a fact may be missing simultaneously. To bridge this gap, we introduce a task called fact generation: generating a valid hyper-relational fact from an arbitrarily masked query, i.e., completing a partially observed fact or generating a fact from scratch. We propose KREPE, the first generative representation learning method for HKGs that learns to model the probability distributions of missing components conditioned on the local fact components and global structure of HKGs via a masked discrete diffusion. KREPE models both the intra-fact dependencies by contextual message passing and inter-fact correlations by aggregating stochastically sampled contexts. KREPE seamlessly unifies link prediction and fact generation within a single training framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance on standard HKG link prediction benchmarks and outperforming LLM-based baselines in generating novel and correct facts.

</details>


### [72] [Riemannian Archetypal Analysis: Interpretable non-linear data analysis on deformed star distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24113)
*Willem Diepeveen,Deanna Needell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动拉回几何的黎曼原型分析（RAM）方法，通过变形星分布和凸松弛-非凸精化优化，在保持经典原型分析可解释性的同时，实现了非线性表达能力，在合成数据和MNIST上展现了有意义的测地线、去噪投影和几何感知分类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典原型分析具有可解释性，但其线性几何在处理强非线性结构数据时性能受限；现有的神经扩展方法虽然提升了灵活性，但往往削弱了原型和插值的几何意义。因此，需要一种既能保持可解释性又能提供非线性表达能力的方法。

Method: 1) 引入变形星分布及其关联的拉回黎曼几何，为流形映射提供统计解释；2) 定义黎曼原型映射（RAM）为投影到原型测地凸组合流形上；3) 提出基于凸松弛后接非凸精化的实用优化方案；4) 提出从数据中学习（通常次优的）变形星分布的学习方案。

Result: 在合成数据和MNIST上的实验表明，该方法能够生成有意义的测地线、有用的去噪投影以及几何感知的分类结果，同时揭示了当前优化的局限性。

Conclusion: 所提出的黎曼原型分析框架成功结合了经典原型分析的可解释性与非线性模型的表达能力，在保持几何意义的同时提升了性能，但优化方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Classical archetypal analysis is appealing for its interpretability, but its linear geometry can limit performance on data with strongly non-linear structure; at the same time, existing neural extensions improve flexibility while often weakening the geometric meaning of archetypes and interpolations. In this work, we develop a Riemannian version of archetypal analysis based on data-driven pullback geometry for real-valued data, with the goal of combining the interpretability of classical archetypal analysis with the expressive power of modern non-linear models. We introduce a class of deformed star distributions together with associated pullback Riemannian geometry to provide a statistical interpretation of the resulting manifold mappings, define the Riemannian archetypal mapping (RAM) as a projection onto the manifold of geodesically convex combinations of archetypes, and propose a practical optimization scheme based on convex relaxation followed by non-convex refinement. We further propose a learning scheme that yields reasonable, albeit generally suboptimal, deformed star distributions from data. Experiments on synthetic examples and MNIST show that the resulting framework produces meaningful geodesics, useful denoising projections, and geometry-aware classifications, while also clarifying where current optimization limitations remain.

</details>


### [73] [Knowledge Graph Modulated Deep Learning for Limited-Sample Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24162)
*Yuwei Xue,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Joseph Liao,Maximilian Diehn,Ash A. Alizadeh,Lei Xing,Md. Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-in-Graph (GiG)框架，将患者数据建模为模块化图结构，其中节点特征来自患者测量值（如基因表达），边由生物知识图谱定义。在近9700名患者、五个临床任务中，GiG一致优于现有方法，尤其在样本有限场景下，前列腺癌诊断任务宏F1提升高达49个百分点。控制实验证实性能提升源于生物学知识图结构而非单纯图建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学AI模型大多依赖压缩的低维表示，无法直接利用图结构化的生物知识，导致信息丢失和性能下降，尤其在有限样本的临床研究中。

Method: GiG框架将每个患者表示为独立的模块化图，使用 curated 生物知识图谱定义边，患者特异性测量值（如基因表达）定义节点特征，从而在患者级表示学习中保留基因-基因相互作用和通路拓扑结构。

Result: 在近9700名患者的五个临床任务中（包括液体活检癌症检测、前列腺癌诊断、32类泛癌分类），GiG一致优于传统及最先进方法；前列腺癌诊断任务中宏F1提升高达49个百分点；随机拓扑控制实验证实性能提升源于生物学知识图谱结构。

Conclusion: 知识图谱调制的深度学习能提高临床数据分析的鲁棒性、可解释性和样本效率，为将生物知识图谱整合到预测建模中提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Biological systems are governed by structured molecular interactions, where pathways, regulatory circuits, and functional gene relationships shape cellular behavior and disease progression. Much of this knowledge is naturally represented as graphs. However, most biomedical AI models cannot directly use graph-encoded biological knowledge and instead require compressed low-dimensional representations, which can lose important structure and reduce performance, especially in limited-sample clinical studies. Here, we introduce Graph-in-Graph (GiG), a knowledge graph-modulated deep learning framework for data-efficient clinical prediction. GiG represents each patient as a standalone modular graph, in which curated biological knowledge graphs define edges and patient-specific measurements, such as gene expression, define node features. This design allows multiple biological knowledge graphs to be integrated while preserving gene-gene interactions and pathway topology during patient-level representation learning. Across cohorts comprising nearly 9,700 patients and five clinical tasks, including liquid biopsy cancer detection, prostate cancer diagnosis, and 32-class pan-cancer classification, GiG consistently outperforms traditional and state-of-the-art methods, with the largest gains in limited-sample settings. On the challenging prostate cancer diagnosis task, GiG improves macro-F1 by up to 49 percentage points relative to competing methods. Control experiments replacing real pathway graphs with random topologies confirm that these gains arise from biologically grounded knowledge graph structure rather than graph modeling alone. These findings show that knowledge graph-modulated deep learning can improve robustness, interpretability, and sample efficiency in clinical data analysis, and provide a principled framework for integrating biological knowledge graphs into predictive modeling.

</details>


### [74] [PromptAudit: Auditing Prompt Sensitivity in LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24171)
*Steffen J. Camarato,Yahya Hmaiti,Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PromptAudit框架通过固定数据集、解码和解析，仅改变提示策略，系统评估了五种提示策略在五个开源模型上对1,000个CVE（6,074个代码样本，16种编程语言）的漏洞检测效果。发现标准思维链提示整体表现最佳，少样本提示对提示敏感模型有提升，而自适应思维链和自一致性方法反而降低性能。提示敏感性是评估和部署中必须显式表征的一阶系统属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在漏洞检测中的可靠性在不同提示形式下缺乏系统刻画，现有研究未将提示效应从其他变量中隔离出来进行控制实验。

Method: 提出PromptAudit控制评估框架，固定数据集、解码方式和解析方法，仅变化提示策略。在五个开源模型上，使用五种提示策略（标准思维链、少样本、自适应思维链、自一致性等）对1,000个CVE（6,074个代码样本，16种编程语言）进行评估，测量准确率、召回率、弃权率、覆盖率和有效F1。

Result: 标准思维链提示在整体操作性能上最强；少样本提示对提示敏感模型有显著但不一致的提升；自适应思维链频繁抑制召回率；自一致性导致过度弃权，大幅降低有效性能。

Conclusion: 漏洞检测行为由模型和提示共同决定，提示敏感性是一阶系统属性，必须在评估和部署中显式表征。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for vulnerability detection, yet their reliability under different prompt formulations remains uncharacterized. We present PromptAudit, a controlled evaluation framework that isolates prompt effects by fixing the dataset, decoding, and parsing while varying only the prompting strategy. Using five prompting strategies across five open-weight models on 1,000 CVEs (6,074 code samples spanning 16 programming languages), we evaluate accuracy, recall, abstention, coverage, and effective F1.
